Unmodified performance gates

The Axum cold and warm time gates fail; cold sampled RSS also fails. PHP comparisons remain unqualified because the isolated metadata omitted original shallow boundaries and yielded no Git-metrics cache proof. All 108 processes complete, and independent full-hash checks confirm one logical graph per project across every run. These are separate performance/metadata gaps, with zero worker or named-source-contract failures.

Predecessor a827759 and current runtime da173ff use identical inputs, owned dependencies, cache defaults and configurations. Three repetitions alternate producer order per project. Each cold, population and warm run starts a fresh process; only the named population/warm cache is shared. Medians must remain at or below 1.10× for both indexing time and sampled process-tree RSS. Population results are retained and required for parity.

RSS is sampled externally every 25 ms across all-thread descendants during indexing. Shared pages may be counted repeatedly, and polling or exiting processes can miss peaks. Parent VmHWM, sampled child peaks, process counts and incomplete samples are retained. The sampler stops and acknowledges its final boundary before logical digest/source verification begins. This is a relative guardrail, not an absolute memory ceiling.

Complete proofs and actual cache behavior

Every original regular file is rehashed against its materialization receipt and Git blob identity before and after each run. Whole logical JSON is hashed in bounded 64 KiB chunks, including every entity, relation, diagnostic, unknown field, condition, source coordinate, repeated proof occurrence and array position. Reviewed equivalence removes only run UUID/time, normalizes Rust symbol producer 2 to 1 and reconstructs exact ordered proofs from file-local tables. Every project must agree across both producers, all modes and all repetitions; independent strict source anchors must also pass.

The working-tree API exercises real caches and includes original Git metrics. Revision mode intentionally disables caching. Isolated owned HEAD/config/index metadata reads original Git objects at the reviewed pins; target Git config, hooks, filters, dependencies and code never execute. Nine original symlinks remain explicitly skipped in the regular-file profile. Missing symlinks can make the isolated working-tree status dirty; that field remains compared. The working-tree graphs include Git metrics, so they are a separate profile from the previous revision-only saved graphs.

Default limits remain two entries per unit, 256 MiB total compressed cache and 64 MiB decoded JSON per entry. A unit exceeding those limits falls back to indexing; warm replay does not imply every analyzer is cached. Actual hit/miss events remain visible above and in the raw receipt. Successful cache bytes are removed only after complete cross-producer/cross-mode parity; their compressed-byte SHA-256/size/name ledgers are retained locally. Failed or unequal units retain their actual cache.

Preserved source gaps

Both PHP cache units decline to store unqualified source results; warm runs reanalyze them. These source failures are distinct from worker failures and remain part of every compared graph.
